公司簡介
COMPANY PROFILE
農夫山泉股份有限公司成立於1996年，為 Established in 1996, Nongfu Spring Co., Ltd. is a leader in the
中國包裝飲用水及飲料的龍頭企業。我們的 packaged drinking water and beverage industry in the PRC. Our
產品主要覆蓋包裝飲用水、茶飲料、功能飲 major products include packaged drinking water, tea beverage,
料及果汁飲料等類別。本公司於2001年6月 functional beverage and juice beverage. The Company was
從有限責任公司整體變更為股份有限公司， transformed from a limited liability company into a joint stock
並於2020年9月在香港聯交所主板上市（股 company with limited liabilities in June 2001 and listed on the Main
份代號：9633.HK）。 Board of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in September 2020 (stock
code: 9633. HK).
我們秉持「天然、健康」的產品理念，堅持使 We believe in offering natural and healthy products and insist on
用優質原料。我們的包裝飲用水全部源自優 using high quality raw materials. All of our packaged drinking water
質天然水源。我們擁有領先的裝備水平、製 products are sourced from quality natural water sources. We
造能力、強大的供應鏈管理能力和嚴格的質 possess industry-leading equipment, manufacturing capabilities,
量保障體系。 strong supply chain management capabilities and rigorous quality
assurance systems.
我們建立了包裝飲用水和飲料的雙引擎發展 We have established dual growth engines, packaged drinking water
格局，擁有覆蓋各省市及縣級行政區域的全 and beverage and set up a nationwide sales network covering all
國性銷售網絡。我們實行多品牌戰略，具有 provinces, municipalities and prefecture-level administrative divisions
長遠的品牌規劃和強大的品牌傳播能力，鑄 across China. We implement a multi-brand strategy, have long-term
就了中國軟飲料的超級品牌。 visions for our brands and strong brand communication capabilities,
and have built a super brand in the PRC soft beverage market.
2
農夫山泉股份有限公司 NONGFU SPRING CO., LTD. 年報 2023 Annual Report

因素交織叠加帶來的下行壓力，經濟運行總 brought by multiple intertwined domestic and international factors,
體回升向好，國內生產總值(GDP)超人民幣 leading to an overall improvement in economic activity, with GDP
126萬億元，比上年增長5.2%。市場活力和 exceeding RMB126 trillion, representing a year-on-year increase

茶飲料產品 Tea beverage products
2023年本集團茶飲料產品通過推陳出新， In 2023, the Group’s tea beverage products continued to grow by
傳播中國茶文化，獲得持續增長，錄得全年 innovating on products and spreading Chinese tea culture, recording
收益人民幣12,659百萬元，較2022年增長 RMB12,659 million of revenue for the year, which indicated an
83.3%，佔2023年總收益的29.7%。 increase of 83.3% as compared to 2022 and accounted for 29.7%
of the total revenue for the year.
報告期內，「東方樹葉」春季限定「龍井新茶」 During the Reporting Period, “Oriental Leaf (東方樹葉)” reintroduced
再度回歸，以其特級龍井的優質原料，打造 “Longjing Spring Tea (龍井新茶)” for the spring season, a premium

環境、社會與管治 ENVIRONMENT, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE
可持續發展一直是農夫山泉秉持的核心發展 As one of the core development concepts upheld by the Company
理念之一，並體現在本公司於環境、社會與 for a long term, sustainable development is reflected in the
管治（下稱「ESG」）各方面的治理、管理與實 governance, management and practices of environment, social and
踐中。本公司已建立可持續發展三級管治架 governance (ESG) issues. The Company has established a three-
構，由公司董事會擔任最高領導者、公共政 level sustainable development structure with the Board acting as the
策與可持續發展辦公室協助董事會管理ESG top leader, the Office of Public Policy & Sustainable Development
事項、各職能部門和附屬公司作為ESG事宜 assisting the Board in managing the ESG issues, and various
執行機構。2023年，本集團在全面瞭解企 functional departments and subsidiaries acting as the executors of
業自身發展狀況的基礎上，廣泛吸納各利益 ESG issues. In 2023, on the basis of comprehensive understanding
相關方訴求與期望，更新了本年度關鍵ESG of business development, the Group conducted an extensive survey
議題，為本公司可持續發展指明方向。 to understand the appeals and expectations of stakeholders, and
updated the key ESG issues for the year accordingly, which set the
direction for sustainable development.

行《反貪污政策》、《舉報政策》和《農夫 and strictly implemented the Anti-Corruption Policy, the
山泉員工商業行為準則》等內部規範， Whistleblowing Policy, the Code of Business Conduct for

股東權利 SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS
單獨或者合計持有公司百分之十以上股份的 The Company shall convene an extraordinary general meeting at the
股東請求時，公司應當召開臨時股東大會。 request of shareholders who individually or collectively hold more
但在公司股東大會決議公告之日或之前，前 than 10% of the Company’s shares. However, prior to the date of
述股東單獨或者合計持有的公司股份不得低 announcement of the resolutions approved at the general meeting,
於公司有表決權股份總數的百分之十；持股 the number of the Company’s shares individually or jointly held by the
比例不足百分之十時，臨時股東大會就前述 above shareholders shall not be lower than 10% of the total number
股東提出的議案所做出的相關決議無效。 of the Company’s shares with voting rights; should the shareholding
is less than 10%, resolutions passed at the extraordinary general
meeting would become invalid.
公司召開股東大會，單獨或合計持有公司有 When the Company convenes a shareholders’ general meeting, the
表決權的股份總數百分之三以上的股東，有 shareholders who individually or jointly, hold more than 3% of the

董事會已按照《企業管治守則》第二部分第 The Board has established a risk management and internal control
D.2段的要求建立了風險管理及內部監控系 system in accordance with the requirements of paragraph D.2 under
統，負責對本公司的風險管理及內部監控系 Section II of the Corporate Governance Code, which is responsible
統的充分性和有效性進行獨立審核，持續監 for independently reviewing the adequacy and effectiveness of the
督並每年至少檢討一次其運行的有效性。該 risk management and internal control system of the Company,
系統旨在管理而非消除未能達成經營目標的 and continuously monitoring and reviewing the effectiveness of its
風險，促進有效及高效運營，合理保證財務 operation once a year. The system aims to manage rather than
報告可靠性及遵守適用法律及法規以及保障 eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, promote
本集團資產。董事會只能就不會有重大的失 effective and efficient operations, reasonably ensure the reliability of
實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的保證。 financial reports and comply with applicable laws and regulations
and protect the assets of the Group. The Board can only give
reasonable but not absolute assurance that there will be no material
misrepresentation or loss.

室和管理層為第二道防綫；審計稽查中心、 The various departments of the Group are the first line of defense

董事會報告
REPORT OF THE DIRECTORS
董事會欣然提呈其報告連同本集團截至 The Board is pleased to present its report together with the audited
2023年12月31日止年度之經審核綜合財務 consolidated financial statements of the Group for the year ended
報表。 December 31, 2023.
主要業務 PRINCIPAL BUSINESS
本集團是中國包裝飲用水及飲料的龍頭企 The Group is a leader in the packaged drinking water and beverage
業，致力於向消費者提供高質量的產品。本 business in the PRC with devotion to providing consumers with high-
集團的主要產品覆蓋包裝飲用水、茶飲料、 quality products. The Group’s major products include packaged
功能飲料及果汁飲料等類別。自1996年成 drinking water, tea beverage, functional beverage and juice beverage.
立以來，本集團即確保「每一滴農夫山泉都 Since its establishment in 1996, the Group has adhered to the
有它的源頭」。本集團的包裝飲用水產品全 principle that “every drop of Nongfu Spring has its source” (每一滴農
部源自天然水源。我們已在中國各不同區域 夫山泉都有它的源頭). The Group sources all of packaged drinking
佈局了十二大優質水源地，全面覆蓋了全國 water products from natural water sources. We have obtained
市場供應。該十二大水源所在地生態環境卓 access to twelve quality natural water sources in different regions
越，水質優異。 of China which cover the entire national market supply. The twelve
water sources are all located in excellent ecological environments
and have outstanding water quality.
本集團建立了包裝飲用水和飲料的雙引擎發 The Group has established dual growth engines, packaged drinking
展格局，既有利於增強我們的市場競爭能力 water and beverage, which enhance our competitive advantage
又能有效降低業績波動風險。本集團實行多 and reduce the risks of fluctuating operating results. The Group
品牌戰略，具有長遠的品牌規劃和強大的品 implements a multi-brand strategy, has long-term visions for our
牌傳播能力，鑄就了中國軟飲料的超級品 brands and strong brand communication capabilities, and has built a
牌。 super brand in the PRC soft beverage market.

1. 公司及集團資料 1. CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION
農夫山泉股份有限公司於2001年6月 Nongfu Spring Co., Ltd. was incorporated and registered in the
27日在中華人民共和國（「中國」）註冊 People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) on 27 June 2001. On 8
成立及登記。於2020年9月8日，本公 September 2020, the Company was listed on the Main Board
司於香港聯合交易所有限公司（「香港 of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “HKSE”)
聯交所」）主板上市（股份代號：9633. (stock code: 9633. HK). The address of the registered office is
HK）。註冊辦事處地址為浙江省杭州 No. 181, Geyazhuang Road, Xihu District, Hangzhou, Zhejiang
市西湖區葛衙莊181號。 Province.
本集團參與以下主要業務： The Group was involved in the following principal activities:
－ 生產及銷售包裝飲用水及飲料 – Production and sale of packaged water and beverage
－ 銷售農產品 – Sale of agricultural products
管理層認為，本公司的控股公司及最 In the opinion of management, the holding company and the
終控股公司為養生堂有限公司，該公 ultimate holding company of the Company is Yangshengtang
司於中國註冊成立。 Co., Ltd., which is incorporated and registered in the PRC.

2. 會計政策 2. ACCOUNTING POLICIES
2.1 編製基準 2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION
該等綜合財務報表乃根據國際財務報 These consolidated financial statements have been prepared
告準則（「國際財務報告準則」）編製， in accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards
當中包括國際會計準則理事會（「國際 (“IFRSs”), which comprise standards and interpretations
會計準則理事會」）批准的準則及詮 approved by the International Accounting Standards Board (the
釋，及國際會計準則理事會批准的國 “IASB”), and International Accounting Standards and Standing
際會計準則及常務詮釋委員會詮釋以 Interpretations Committee interpretations approved by the
及香港公司條例之披露規定。 International Accounting Standards Board and the disclosure
requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.
綜合財務報表乃按照歷史成本原則編 The consolidated financial statements have been prepared
製。除有特別註明外，該等財務報表 under the historical cost convention. These financial statements
以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，且所有數 are presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded
值均約整至最近的千元單位。 to the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.
綜合基準 Basis of consolidation
綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公 The consolidated financial statements include the financial
司（統稱「本集團」）於截至2023年12月 statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively
31日止年度的財務報表。附屬公司為 referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December
本公司直接或間接控制的實體（包括結 2023. A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity),
構性實體）。當本集團對參與被投資方 directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control
業務的可變回報承擔風險或享有權利 is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to
以及能透過其權力影響被投資方的回 variable returns from its involvement with the investee and has
報時（即賦予本集團現有能力主導被投 the ability to affect those returns through its power over the
資方相關活動的既存權利），即取得控 investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the current
制權。 ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee).
於一般情況下均存在多數投票權形成 Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting
控制權之推定。倘本公司擁有少於被 rights results in control. When the Company has less than a
投資方過半數投票或類似權利，則本 majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group
集團於評估其是否對被投資方擁有權 considers all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing
力時會考慮一切相關事實及情況，包 whether it has power over an investee, including:
括：
(a) 與被投資方其他投票權持有人的 (a) the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders
合約安排； of the investee;
(b) 其他合約安排產生的權利；及 (b) rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and
(c) 本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。 (c) the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.
附屬公司的財務報表乃就與本公司相 The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared
同的報告期間採用一致的會計政策編 for the same reporting period as the Company, using
製。附屬公司的業績自本集團取得控 consistent accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries
制權當日起綜合入賬，並繼續綜合入 are consolidated from the date on which the Group obtains
賬直至有關控制權終止當日為止。 control, and continue to be consolidated until the date that
such control ceases.

2.1 編製基準（續） 2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (continued)
綜合基準（續） Basis of consolidation (continued)
即使會導致非控股權益產生虧絀結 Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive
餘，損益及其他全面收益各組成部分 income are attributed to the owners of the parent of the Group
仍會歸屬於本集團母公司擁有人及非 and to the non-controlling interests, even if this results in the
控股權益。所有與本集團成員公司之 non-controlling interests having a deficit balance. All intra-
間交易有關的集團內公司間的資產及 group assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses and cash
負債、權益、收益、開支及現金流量 flows relating to transactions between members of the Group
均於綜合入賬時悉數對銷。 are eliminated in full on consolidation.

金融資產減值 Impairment of financial assets
本集團就並非按公平值計入損益持有 The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses
的所有債務工具確認預期信貸虧損 (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through
（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期信貸虧 profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the
損乃基於根據合約到期的合約現金流 contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract
量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量 and all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive,
之間的差額而釐定，並以原實際利率 discounted at an approximation of the original effective interest
的近似值貼現。預期現金流量將包括 rate. The expected cash flows will include cash flows from the
來自出售所持抵押品或組成合約條款 sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements that are
的其他信貸提升措施的現金流量。 integral to the contractual terms.
一般方法 General approach
預期信貸虧損於兩個階段確認。就自 ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for
初步確認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加 which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk
的信貸風險而言，本集團會為未來12 since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses
個月可能發生的違約事件所產生的信 that result from default events that are possible within the next
貸虧損（12個月預期信貸虧損）計提 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for
預期信貸虧損撥備。對於自初步確認 which there has been a significant increase in credit risk since
後有顯著增加的信貸風險，須在信貸 initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses
虧損風險預期的剩餘年期計提虧損撥 expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective
備，不論違約事件於何時發生（全期預 of the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).
期信貸虧損）。
於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具 At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the

努力即可獲得的合理及可靠資料，包 initial recognition and considers reasonable and supportable
括歷史及前瞻性資料。本集團認為， information that is available without undue cost or effort,

ESTIMATES
編製本集團的財務報表需要管理層作 The preparation of the Group’s financial statements requires
出判斷、估計及假設，而該等判斷、 management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions
估計及假設會影響收益、開支、資產 that affect the reported amounts of revenues, expenses, assets
及負債的呈報金額及其隨附披露以及 and liabilities, and their accompanying disclosures, and the
或然負債的披露。該等假設及估計的 disclosure of contingent liabilities. Uncertainty about these
不確定性可導致需要對未來受影響的 assumptions and estimates could result in outcomes that
資產或負債的賬面值作重大調整的後 could require a material adjustment to the carrying amounts of
果。 the assets or liabilities affected in the future.
判斷 Judgement
於應用本集團會計政策的過程中，除 In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,
涉及估計的判斷外，管理層已作出以 management has made the following judgements, apart from
下對財務報表內確認的金額有最重大 those involving estimations, which have the most significant
影響力的判斷： effect on the amounts recognised in the financial statements:
遞延稅項資產 Deferred tax assets
未動用的稅項虧損及可扣減暫時差額 Deferred tax assets are recognised for unused tax losses
應確認為遞延稅項資產，惟限於有可 and deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is
能動用該虧損及可扣減暫時差額以供 probable that taxable profit will be available against which the
抵銷將會錄得的應課稅溢利。確認遞 losses and deductible temporary differences can be utilised.

生之時間及未來應課稅溢利之數量連 the amount of deferred tax assets that can be recognised,
同未來課稅規劃策略作出重大判斷。 based upon the likely timing and level of future taxable profits
進一步詳情載列於財務報表附註17。 together with future tax planning strategies. Further details are

銷售獎勵折扣的可變代價 Variable consideration for volume rebates
本集團估計將計入附有銷售獎勵折扣 The Group estimates variable consideration to be included
的產品銷售交易價格的可變代價。 in the transaction price for the sales of products with volume
rebates.
本集團就設有單一數量限額的合約按 The Group’s volume rebates are analysed on a per customer
每名客戶作基準分析銷售獎勵折扣。 basis for contracts that are subject to a single volume
釐定客戶是否可能有權獲得折扣取決 threshold. Determining whether a customer will likely be
於客戶過往享有的折扣及迄今的累計 entitled to a rebate depends on the customer’s historical
採購量。 rebate entitlement and accumulated purchases to date.
本集團每年更新其對預期銷售獎勵折 The Group updates its assessment of expected volume
扣的評估，並相應調整應付銷售折扣 rebates yearly and the sales discount and rebate payables are
及獎勵折扣。預期獎勵折扣的估計對 adjusted accordingly. Estimates of expected volume rebates
情況變動敏感，而本集團有關獎勵折 are sensitive to changes in circumstances and the Group’s
扣配額的過往經驗未必能代表客戶日 past experience regarding rebate entitlements may not be
後的實際獎勵折扣配額。 representative of a customer’s actual rebate entitlements in the

29. 員工股權激勵計劃 29. EMPLOYEE SHARE INCENTIVE SCHEME
經2022年1月14日召開的臨時股東大 As approved in the extraordinary general meeting held on
會審議通過，本公司已採納員工股權 14 January 2022, the Company has adopted the Employee
激勵計劃（「該計劃」），以激勵對本集 Share Incentive Scheme (the “Scheme”) to incentivize
團經營業績和未來發展發揮重要作用 management personnel and core technical experts who play
的管理人員和核心技術專家，包括(i)本 an important role in the Group’s business performance and
集團的董事、監事和員工；以及(ii)在 future development, including (i) the Directors, Supervisors

30. 儲備 30. RESERVES
本集團的儲備金額及其變動於財務報 The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements
表的綜合權益變動表呈列。 therein are presented in the consolidated statement of changes
in equity in the financial statements.
(a) 法定儲備 (a) Statutory reserve
根據中華人民共和國公司法，中 In accordance with the Company Law of the People’s
國公司須將法定除稅後利潤的 Republic of China, the companies in the PRC are
10%轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲備 required to allocate 10% of the statutory after tax profits
的累計總額達到公司註冊資本的 to the statutory reserve until the cumulative total of
50%。待中國有關部門批准後， the reserve reaches 50% of the companies’ registered
法定儲備可用於抵銷任何累計虧 capital. Subject to the approval of the relevant PRC
損或增加公司的註冊資本。法定 authorities, the statutory reserve may be used to offset
儲備不可用於向中國附屬公司股 any accumulated losses or increase the registered capital
東分派股息。 of the companies. The statutory reserve is not available
for dividend distribution to shareholders of the PRC
subsidiaries.
(b) 資本儲備 (b) Capital reserve
本集團的資本儲備指最終控股公 The capital reserve of the Group represents the
司及股東的貢獻及代價超出所收 contribution from the ultimate holding company and
購非控股權益的賬面值的部分。 shareholders and the excess of the consideration over
the carrying amount of the non-controlling interests
acquired.
(c) 匯兌波動儲備 (c) Exchange fluctuation reserve
匯兌波動儲備指因換算其功能貨 The exchange fluctuation reserve represents exchange
幣與本集團呈列貨幣不同的海外 differences arising from the translation of the financial
業務的財務報表而產生的匯兌差 statements of foreign operations whose functional
額。 currencies are different from the Group’s presentation
currency.
(d) 股份支付儲備 (d) Share-based payment reserve
股份支付儲備包括尚未歸屬之股 The share-based payment reserve comprises the fair
份獎勵公平值，詳見財務報表附 value of shares awarded which are yet to be vested, as
註2.4有關股份支付之會計政策。 further explained in the accounting policy for share-based
payments in note 2.4 to the financial statements.

資本管理 Capital management
本集團資本管理的主要目標為確保本 The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are
集團持續經營的能力以及維持穩健的 to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern
資本比率，以支持其業務及為股東爭 and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its
取最大價值。 business and maximise shareholders’ value.
本集團考慮經濟狀況變化及相關資產 The Group manages its capital structure and makes
的風險特徵來管理其資本架構並作出 adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions
相應調整。為維持或調整資本架構， and the risk characteristics of the underlying assets. To
本集團或會調整支付予股東的股息、 maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust
退回股本予股東或發行新股。本集團 the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital to
不受任何外部資本限制的約束。年 shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not subject to
內，本集團的資本管理目標、政策或 any externally imposed capital requirements. No changes were
程序未發生變化。 made in the objectives, policies or processes for managing
capital during the year.
本集團採用資本負債比率監控資本， The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is debt
該比率按債務除以經調整資本計算。 divided by the adjusted capital. Debt includes interest-bearing
債務包括計息借貸以及租賃負債。資 borrowings and lease liabilities. Capital represents equity
本指母公司擁有人應佔權益。於報告 attributable to owners of the parent. The gearing ratios as at
期末的資本負債比率如下： the end of the reporting periods were as follows:
